HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001251/B001","root_001395/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0860f7dbb605fe645211","text":"1. **Apparent extension bounded by actual fewness.** **م ت ع B001/B007** supplies enjoyed benefit and prolonged keeping; **ق ل ل B001** fewness. The verb can suggest extension, but its adverb immediately contracts the scale. I infer that experiential duration can feel stretched while remaining structurally small relative to what follows. The line destabilizes enjoyment’s own measure rather than denying that it is enjoyed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000907/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3a5b39876a6bbb34e935","text":"2. **Voluntary attachment reversed into forced conveyance.** Lexical evidence: `ض ر ر B003` is compulsion under necessity, `ع ذ ب B005` is punishment, and `غ ل ظ B001/B002` is material thickness and harsh severity. Structural cue: 31:22 described voluntary handover and a firm grip; here the subject is compelled `إِلَىٰ` a punishment whose adjective gives it resistant density. Assigned role: compulsion supplies motion without consent, and thickness makes the destination hard, enclosing, and difficult to pass through. Abductive move (inferred): refusal to adopt the reliable attachment leaves only externally imposed direction at the endpoint. Reading change: the punishment is read spatially as forced entry into a dense constraint, not merely increased intensity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000907/B003","root_000907/B005","root_000994/B005","root_001099/B001","root_001099/B002","root_001395/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_58461e31e0432a0dbcd1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"31:24:1\",\"root_001395\",[\"B003\"]],[\"31:24:4\",\"root_000907\",[\"B003\",\"B005\"]],[\"31:24:6\",\"root_000994\",[\"B005\"]],[\"31:24:7\",\"root_001099\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kullanılabilir serbestlik, zorunlu yönelişe ve giderek kalınlaşan sert bir sınıra dönüşerek hareket alanını aşamalı biçimde kapatır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000907/B003","root_000994/B005","root_001099/B001","root_001395/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5edf48d4a86cfc75f5a6","text":"2. **Wide use narrows into compelled edge.** **م ت ع B003** is usable provision; **ض ر ر B003/B005** is compulsion under necessity and constrained nearness at an edge; **ع ذ ب B005** punishment; **غ ل ظ B001–B002** material thickness and rough severity. The sequence moves from discretionary use to forced direction and finally a resistant, thick object. I infer progressive loss of maneuvering space. Punishment reads as an approach whose constraints thicken until alternatives disappear.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7b73ef5b65d1c828bb11","text":"Ayah 31:33 explained the perceptual mechanism behind “a little”: near life deceives through first appearance and incompletion, while the firm promise supplies the larger measure. Temporary latitude can therefore feel total only by hiding its outcome.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001395/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_817766c75d921552d58d","text":"1. **Enjoyment reclassified as bounded deferment.** Lexical evidence: `م ت ع B001` is benefit enjoyed and used, `م ت ع B007` is prolonged keeping so benefit can continue, and `ق ل ل B001` is fewness or smallness. Structural cue: enjoyment occupies the first interval, but `ثُمَّ` moves to a radically different endpoint; 31:29 will give every course a fixed term. Assigned role: benefit makes the interval genuinely usable, while littleness measures it against what follows. Abductive move (inferred): temporal extension does not imply endorsement when the same sequence announces its boundary and outcome. Reading change: present enjoyment becomes respite with a limit, not evidence that denial has succeeded.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001251/B001","root_001395/B001","root_001395/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b41984089acf1b4c7589","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"31:24:1\",\"root_001395\",[\"B001\",\"B007\"]],[\"31:24:2\",\"root_001251\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yararlanma deneyim içinde uzun görünebilse de ardından gelen sonuçla ölçüldüğünde yapısal olarak kısa bir ertelemedir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e3b6eb2246ccc3a8e347","text":"- 31:29's `أَجَلٍ مُّسَمًّى` supplied the later mechanism that bounds `قَلِيلًا`: a course may continue usefully while still moving toward an assigned endpoint.
